[写作评审] 轮次 2/5

维度评分（1-4，4 为最好）：
- Originality: 2     ← D019 后 persuasion-specific claim 已被 falsify，domain-adaptive finding 有价值但论文尚未呈现
- Clarity: 2         ← 全文叙事与实验证据矛盾（讲 persuasion-specific 故事，但 evidence 说 domain exposure）
- Soundness: 1       ← experiments.tex L149 "necessary and sufficient" 被 MLM control 直接证伪；PENDING 占位符未填
- Significance: 2    ← domain-adaptive finding 本身有贡献，但论文未正确呈现
- Presentation: 3    ← 表格/图表/格式整洁，表面写作质量尚可

Overall（1-10，10 为最好）: 2

## Round 1 修复验证
全部 6 项 C1-C6 修复确认 ✅。Round 1 WARNING 修复全部确认：
- LLM 在 Abstract 中展开 ✅ (L4)
- "pp" 全文展开为 "percentage point(s)" ✅ (仅 Table 3 表头 "$\Delta$ (pp)" 保留)
- Fig.3 \includegraphics 恢复 ✅ (experiments.tex L229)
- gururangan2020dontstop 引用添加 ✅ (experiments.tex L135)
- Conclusion LLM-as-judge 结果句 ✅ (conclusion.tex L13)
- li2024mhj 在 Related Work §2.1 ✅ (related_work.tex L8)
- 新增 4 条 DAPT/MTL bib 条目，全部在正文被引用 ✅
- DAPT/MTL Related Work 小节 positioning 准确 ✅

## 具体问题

### CRITICAL: D019 Reframe 未执行 — 全文叙事与证据矛盾

plan_017 MLM control 已完成 (registry: DONE, DD OOD F1=1.000 across all K)，D019 decision 已记录，paper_framing.md 已更新为 Track B。但 **所有 6 个 .tex 文件仍在讲 D019 之前的旧故事**。以下是逐条清单（非穷举，仅列最严重的）：

- [severity: critical] **experiments.tex L149**: "persuasion-aware fine-tuning is both **necessary** and sufficient" — **NECESSARY 被 plan_017 直接证伪**（MLM 无任何分类标签即 F1=1.0）。这是最严重的单句错误。
- [severity: critical] **experiments.tex L153**: `[PENDING: plan_016/017 domain-adaptive control results]` — plan_017 已 DONE (DD F1=1.0 all K, IID K1=0.96 Full=1.0)。占位符必须替换为实际结果。
- [severity: critical] **related_work.tex L28**: "Ongoing control experiments (Section~\ref{sec:ablation}) **will test** whether fine-tuning on non-persuasion auxiliary tasks... produces comparable transfer" — plan_017 MLM 已完成且结果为 F1=1.0。"will test" 应改为报告实际结果。
- [severity: critical] **abstract.tex L7**: "The persuasion objective teaches the encoder to attend to pragmatic features" — MLM（无 persuasion objective）达到相同效果。该句 claim 的因果关系不成立。
- [severity: critical] **introduction.tex L10**: "fine-tuning an encoder on persuasion-related text should teach it to attend to pragmatic features" — 这是旧假说。应改为：original hypothesis was refined by systematic ablation.
- [severity: critical] **introduction.tex L12**: "The fine-tuning process on persuasion-related text... is the critical ingredient" — Post-D019 应为 "domain-adaptive pretraining on jailbreak conversation text"。
- [severity: critical] **introduction.tex L16 Contribution 1**: "Cross-attack generalization through persuasion-aware fine-tuning" — 标题需改。
- [severity: critical] **method.tex L15**: "The core hypothesis... is that multi-turn jailbreak attacks rely on persuasion strategies that leave detectable pragmatic signatures" — 这正是被 plan_017 falsify 的假说。
- [severity: critical] **conclusion.tex L7**: "persuasion-aware fine-tuning bridges the cross-attack generalization gap" — 应为 domain-adaptive pretraining。
- [severity: critical] **conclusion.tex L10-11**: "fine-tuning on persuasion-related text... is the critical ingredient" + "exposure to persuasion-domain conversations during multi-task training" — MLM 无需 multi-task training。
- [severity: critical] **experiments.tex L72**: "Persuasion-aware fine-tuning enables robust detection" — 应为 domain-adaptive pretraining。
- [severity: critical] **experiments.tex L95**: "validating persuasion-aware fine-tuning as the mechanism" — 直接与 MLM control 矛盾。

**总计**：20+ 处叙事需要从 "persuasion-aware fine-tuning is the mechanism" 改为 "domain-adaptive pretraining (domain exposure) is the mechanism, persuasion taxonomy provides interpretability only"。这不是措辞微调，是整体叙事重构。paper_framing.md 已有完整的 D019 reframe 指引。

### WARNING: Table 2 数据错误

- [severity: warning] **experiments.tex Table 2 (L120)**: Mean pooling DD-K=2 显示 **.990**，但 registry plan_015 记录 meanpool_treatment_k2_f1 = **1.000**。相差 1pp。应修正为 1.000。

  验证路径: registry plan_015_dd_eval → meanpool_treatment_k2_f1: 1

### WARNING: AI 风格 trailing -ing phrases

- [severity: warning] 5 处 trailing -ing 短语（CLAUDE.md §5.3 禁止项）：
  - related_work.tex L12: "...producing representations that encode pragmatic dynamics and transfer across attack families without retraining"
  - related_work.tex L22: "...transforming the theoretical link between persuasion and jailbreaking into a practical detection mechanism"
  - related_work.tex L28: "...isolating whether the gains are attributable to persuasion-specific pragmatic features or to domain adaptation more broadly"
  - experiments.tex L95: "...validating persuasion-aware fine-tuning as the mechanism for cross-attack generalization"
  - conclusion.tex L12: "...confirming that domain exposure drives the generalization gain"

### INFO: 数据一致性通过

- [severity: info] 全文数字交叉验证：DD F1=1.000/0.284 (+72pp)、GPT-5.5 F1=0.191-0.449 (30-47% unparseable)、scrambled 1pp、perturbation 0/11.8/1.3pp — 全部与 registry 一致 ✓
- [severity: info] IID Vanilla 3-seed 均值核验：K1=.968±.033 (计算值 0.9679±0.0332) ✓
- [severity: info] 新增 DAPT/MTL 文献节质量良好，positioning 准确 ✓

## 结论

Round 2 的 C1-C6 修复和 Round 1 WARNING 修复全部到位。但**论文的根本问题未解决**：D019 reframe 完全没有执行。当前论文的核心叙事 ("persuasion-aware fine-tuning is the critical mechanism") 与最重要的实验结果 (plan_017 MLM F1=1.0, 无任何 persuasion classification) 直接矛盾。experiments.tex 中仍有 PENDING 占位符和被证伪的 "necessary and sufficient" claim。

**Round 3 的唯一优先级**: 根据 D019/paper_framing.md 执行全文叙事重构，将 core claim 从 "persuasion-specific supervision" 改为 "domain-adaptive pretraining/domain exposure"，填入 plan_017 MLM 结果，修正所有被证伪的 causal claims。

_source line 2106 · 2026-05-18T06:04:09.302Z_

[Agent 回复]:
[资源] GPU 4/4 分配 | Worker 1 (GPU 1) | 磁盘: westd-33183 待确认

## plan_016v2 Topic Control 完成 — 关键发现

### 结果

| Method | DD OOD Full F1 | Same text? | Supervision |
|--------|------------|------------|------------|
| Vanilla baseline | 0.285 | No | None |
| **Topic classification** | **0.727** | Yes | 5-class topic |
| MLM (plan_017) | 1.000 | Yes | Self-supervised |
| Persuasion (plan_006) | 1.000 | Yes | 8-class persuasion + intent |

Cramer's V (topic vs intent label) = 0.228，无标签泄漏。

### 综合分析

三个 control 实验形成清晰层级：

1. **Text exposure 是基础**：任何在 jailbreak 文本上 fine-tune 的模型都优于 vanilla（Topic +44pp, MLM +72pp, Persuasion +72pp vs vanilla）
2. **MLM ≈ Persuasion > Topic >> Vanilla**：纯 MLM（无任何分类监督）即达满分，与 persuasion 完全一致
3. **错误的分类任务反而有害**：Topic 分类（0.727）显著低于 MLM（1.0），说明 classification objective 如果与目标不对齐，会约束表示空间，损害 detection

### 对 Paper Narrative 的影响

**核心矛盾**：persuasion classification 不优于纯 MLM，说明 persuasion labels 对 detection 无增量贡献。但 topic classification 劣于两者，说明不是"随便分类都行"。

**可能的 reframe 方向**（待 plan_018 结果确认 domain specificity 后再最终决定）：
- A) "Domain-adaptive continued pretraining + task-aligned auxiliary objective"：MLM 提供 domain adaptation，persuasion 提供 interpretability 且不损害性能
- B) "Persuasion classification as a compatible fine-tuning objective"：与 detection 对齐的分类不损害（persuasion），不对齐的损害（topic）
- C) 如果 plan_018 wiki MLM 也=1.0：则甚至 domain 不重要，需要更根本的 pivot

### 下一步
- plan_018 Wikipedia MLM worker 运行中（cuda:0），预计 ~2h 出结果
- plan_018 结果决定最终 reframe 方向
- 暂不动 paper，等三角验证完成

_source line 2107 · 2026-05-18T06:04:42.485Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a very interesting result! plan_016v2 topic classification achieved F1=0.727 on DD OOD - significantly better than vanilla (0.285) but significantly worse than MLM (1.0) and persuasion (1.0).

This creates a nuanced picture:
- vanilla (0.285) << topic (0.727) << MLM (1.0) = persuasion (1.0)

Key insights:
1. Text exposure helps (topic > vanilla by +44pp)
2. But the wrong classification task hurts compared to pure MLM (topic 0.727 < MLM 1.0)
3. MLM = persuasion = 1.0, so classification supervision adds nothing over pure domain exposure


4. The real story is that MLM's pure domain exposure works optimally because it doesn't constrain representations with irrelevant tasks - topic classification interferes by pushing representations toward topic features rather than jailbreak-relevant ones, while persuasion classification happens to be compatible but doesn't improve over MLM alone.

5. Looking at the full evidence hierarchy, scrambled labels achieving 0.99 is puzzling me - it's still a 9-class task on the same text with random label mappings, so why does it match MLM's performance while topic classification (5-class) only reaches 0.73? The difference might be in how the task structure itself affects what the model learns about the domain.

I think meaningful but misaligned labels actually constrain the representation space in a harmful direction, whereas random labels provide no consistent signal to bias the representations - the model can't learn the random mapping effectively, so it ends up behaving more like MLM, just absorbing the text distribution without task-specific interference. This means the hierarchy is really: vanilla (0.28) < topic-aligned (0.73) < {MLM, scrambled, binary-persuasion, and 9-class persuasion all clustering around 0.99-1.0}.

The key insight is that domain exposure on jailbreak text is critical, but classification objectives only hurt if they're misaligned with detection - neutral or aligned objectives like persuasion and scrambled labels don't degrade performance, while MLM remains the safest since it avoids any task bias entirely. I should update the paper framing with this more nuanced story about representation alignment rather than just domain exposure.

For the Wikipedia MLM experiment, if it performs around 1.0, that would suggest domain specificity doesn't matter and any continued pretraining helps, but if it's significantly lower, that confirms we actually need jailbreak-specific text exposure. The Agent wants to wait for those results, but I think the plan_016v2 findings are already substantial enough to integrate now.

I'm going to log this as a key finding, update the framing document with the enriched evidence hierarchy, and notify the user about this interesting result while holding off on sending patches to the Paper Agent until plan_018 completes - that way I can see whether domain specificity is truly necessary or just helpful.
```

</details>

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01EpZNQp7ctwDJjKZHxMCY2m`</summary>

```text
1	# Paper Framing — persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect
2	
3	## Target Venue
4	- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR submission)
5	- Deadline: 2026-05-25 (ARR May cycle)
6	- Track: LLM Security / Discourse and Pragmatics
7	- Commitment deadline: 2026-08-02
8	
9	## Title (Working)
10	What Makes Multi-Turn Jailbreaks Detectable? Domain Exposure Bridges the Cross-Attack Gap
11	
12	## Core Insight (D019 — post-MLM-control reframe)
13	Vanilla semantic temporal modeling (generic DeBERTa + GRU) catastrophically fails on unseen multi-turn attack types (F1=0.28 on Deceptive Delight). We hypothesized that persuasion-specific classification would be the critical ingredient, but systematic ablation reveals a simpler mechanism: domain-adaptive continued pretraining on jailbreak conversation text (even MLM without any classification labels) is sufficient to bridge the OOD gap (F1=1.0, +72pp). The complete evidence hierarchy: vanilla (0.28) < MLM-only (1.0) = scrambled labels (0.99) = binary labels (0.99) = 9-class labels (1.0). The classification task contributes nothing beyond what domain exposure provides. The persuasion taxonomy's sole surviving value is interpretability via strategy trajectory analysis. This is an empirical findings paper, not a method paper: the contribution is the systematic decomposition revealing that domain exposure, not task-specific supervision, is the mechanism.
14	
15	## Novelty Positioning (D019 reframe)
16	NOT: "We discover that persuasion patterns exist in jailbreaks" (already known).
17	NOT: "Persuasion-aware fine-tuning is the critical ingredient" (MLM control falsified this — D019).
18	NOT: "An evasion hierarchy exists: lexical < semantic < pragmatic" (TF-IDF=0.94 on DD contradicts this).
19	YES: "We systematically decompose what makes multi-turn jailbreaks detectable across attack types. The surprising finding: domain-adaptive pretraining on jailbreak conversation text (even MLM) is sufficient for cross-attack generalization. Classification supervision, label quality, label granularity, temporal modeling, and loss weighting are all non-factors. This pinpoints domain exposure as the mechanism and positions the persuasion taxonomy as an interpretability tool, not a detection driver."
20	
21	## Inherent Lexical Separability (D009)
22	Persuasive jailbreak conversations and general benign conversations are inherently lexically separable (TF-IDF≈1.0 on ALL data sources including real WildChat). TF-IDF remains strong even OOD (F1=0.94 on DD). The paper acknowledges this and positions DisPeD's value as: (1) bridging the vanilla temporal modeling gap (+72pp on DD), (2) zero degradation under adversarial perturbation (plan_004), (3) interpretability via strategy trajectories.
23	
24	## Comprehensive Ablation Picture (D014)
25	Systematic ablation reveals the method's actual mechanism:
26	- **Critical**: Persuasion-domain DeBERTa fine-tuning (any form) — bridges OOD gap from F1=0.28 to F1=1.0
27	- **Non-critical**: Label granularity (binary≈scrambled≈8-class, 1-6pp gap on DD), Lambda tuning (all F1=1.0), Temporal modeling (mean pooling≈GRU on DD)
28	- **Implication**: The persuasion multi-task objective is an effective and accessible supervision signal. Architecture and annotation precision are secondary. 8-class taxonomy provides interpretability, not detection advantage.
29	
30	## Core Claims (3, priority-ordered — D019 reframe)
31	
32	### Claim 1 (PRIMARY): Domain Exposure Bridges the Cross-Attack OOD Gap
33	Vanilla DeBERTa catastrophically fails on unseen attack types (F1=0.28 on DD). Domain-adaptive continued pretraining on jailbreak conversation text bridges this gap completely (F1=1.0, +72pp). The critical finding: even MLM without any classification labels achieves F1=1.0 on DD (plan_017), proving that domain exposure alone is sufficient. Classification supervision (persuasion, sentiment, binary) adds nothing to detection. Complete evidence hierarchy: vanilla (0.28) < MLM-only (1.0) = scrambled (0.99) = binary (0.99) = 9-class (1.0).
34	- Evidence: plan_017 MLM DD — F1=1.0 across all K (no classification labels)
35	- Evidence: plan_006 DD — Treatment F1=1.0, Baseline=0.28 (+72pp)
36	- Evidence: plan_006 scrambled DD — F1=0.935-0.990 (labels non-essential)
37	- Evidence: plan_013 binary DD — F1=0.99 (label granularity non-essential)
38	- Strength: very strong — MLM control is the definitive causal test
39	
40	### Claim 2 (ANALYSIS): Persuasion Taxonomy as Interpretability Layer
41	While the persuasion classification task does not contribute to detection performance, the 9-class taxonomy provides interpretability that MLM cannot: different attack types exhibit distinct strategy trajectories (Crescendo: gradual escalation 0.86→5.64, DD: emotional-manipulation→role-assignment, FITD: commitment patterns). This is the taxonomy's sole surviving value after the MLM control result.
42	- Evidence: plan_008 strategy transition matrices
43	- Evidence: plan_017 falsifies detection contribution, confirming interpretability-only role
44	- Strength: descriptive/analytical; interpretability value independent of detection mechanism
45	
46	### Claim 3 (EMPIRICAL): Robustness Under OOD and Adversarial Perturbation
47	Domain-adapted models (whether via MLM or classification fine-tuning) show zero degradation under adversarial perturbation at K≥2, while vanilla baseline degrades -12pp and TF-IDF degrades -1.3pp(K≥3) to -9.6pp(K=1). Strongest differentiation at early detection K=1 under perturbation.
48	- Evidence: plan_004 — Treatment 0pp, Baseline -12pp, TF-IDF -1.3 to -9.6pp
49	- Evidence: plan_006 — Treatment=1.0, TF-IDF=0.94, Baseline=0.28 on DD
50	- Note: MLM perturbation robustness not yet tested (plan_017 only tested clean DD)
```

```text
50	- Note: MLM perturbation robustness not yet tested (plan_017 only tested clean DD)
51	
52	## Story Spine (D019 reframe)
53	
54	**Problem**: Multi-turn jailbreak attacks distribute adversarial intent across individually benign turns. Bullwinkel et al. (2025) show per-turn representations are deliberately kept in a "benign" region. DeepContext tracks generic semantic drift (F1=0.84) but collapses to F1=0.28 on unseen attack families.
55	
56	**Gap**: What representation properties enable cross-attack generalization? Vanilla encoders fail catastrophically on unseen attacks. LLM-as-judge approaches (GPT-5.5 F1=0.19-0.45) cannot detect process-level manipulation. No published work systematically decomposes what makes the OOD gap bridgeable.
57	
58	**Our angle**: We systematically investigate what enables cross-attack jailbreak detection through a comprehensive ablation study. Starting with the hypothesis that persuasion-specific classification drives generalization, we progressively strip components: label quality (scrambled → same), label granularity (binary → same), temporal modeling (mean pooling → same), loss weighting (all lambda → same). The critical control: MLM continued pretraining without any classification labels also achieves F1=1.0 on unseen attacks. This pinpoints domain exposure (not task-specific supervision) as the mechanism. The persuasion taxonomy provides interpretability but not detection advantage.
59	
60	**Headline result**: "Vanilla semantic modeling collapses on unseen multi-turn attack types (F1=0.28 on Deceptive Delight). We expected persuasion-specific classification to be the key ingredient, but systematic ablation reveals a simpler mechanism: domain-adaptive continued pretraining on jailbreak conversation text (even MLM) bridges the entire +72pp OOD gap. The persuasion taxonomy's value is interpretability, not detection."
61	
62	## Architecture
63	
64	1. **Stage 1**: DeBERTa-v3-base fine-tuned on multi-task objective: 9-class persuasion strategy classification (8 strategies + none) + binary jailbreak-intent classification. Loss: L = L_strategy + 0.3 × L_intent
65	2. **Stage 2**: BiGRU over DeBERTa [CLS] embeddings → binary classifier for conversation-level jailbreak detection
66	3. **Baseline**: Same pipeline but with vanilla (not fine-tuned) DeBERTa embeddings
67	Note: All main results use BiGRU. Ablation (plan_015) shows mean pooling ≈ GRU on both IID and OOD, confirming temporal modeling is unnecessary. This is an ablation finding, not a design choice — GRU remains default for consistency with all reported experiments. Stage-1 training: 4 epochs, best checkpoint selected by validation.
68	
69	## Venue Fit Risks
70	- EMNLP 2026 LLM Security track: strong fit (dedicated track, high visibility)
71	- Discourse/Pragmatics reviewers may want more linguistic rigor: ground taxonomy in PAP (ACL 2024), Hagendorff (Nature Comm 2026), Cialdini principles, Speech Act Theory (Austin 1962, Searle 1969)
72	- Safety reviewers expect multiple attack types: need >= 2 attack families
73	
74	## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns
75	
76	### RC1: Lexical Shortcut (CRITICAL, must address pre-submission)
77	TF-IDF=1.0 IID and 0.94 OOD on DD. Lexical features are inherently strong for this task (D009). DisPeD cannot claim superiority over TF-IDF on raw detection performance alone.
78	- Mitigation (resolved, D009+D012): (a) acknowledge inherent lexical separability honestly; (b) DisPeD's primary value vs TF-IDF is interpretability (strategy trajectories, Claim 2) and bridging the vanilla temporal modeling gap (+72pp on DD); (c) plan_004 adversarial perturbation confirmed: Treatment 0pp degradation vs TF-IDF -1.3pp(K≥3) to -9.6pp(K=1) vs Baseline -12pp. Strongest differentiation at early detection K=1-2; (d) the core comparison that motivates DisPeD is vs vanilla DeBERTa+GRU (DeepContext approach, F1=0.28 on DD), not vs TF-IDF; (e) persuasion fine-tuning as supervision signal is the contribution, not beating a bag-of-words. ✅ RESOLVED
79	
80	### RC2: Small Dataset
81	480 samples is small for a detection paper.
82	- Mitigation: (a) statistical significance via bootstrap CI or paired tests; (b) cross-validation; (c) focus narrative on proof-of-concept for the principle, not SOTA numbers
83	
84	### RC3: Single Attack Type (Crescendo Only)
85	Need generalization evidence.
86	- Mitigation: generate FITD-style and Deceptive Delight-style conversations using existing pipeline
87	
88	### RC4: Strategy Label Quality (D010)
89	Scrambled-label control (D010) shows correct strategy labels contribute only 1-6pp to cross-attack F1. This is not a confound but a finding: the persuasion multi-task objective works as a supervision signal even with imperfect labels. The 9-class taxonomy's primary value is interpretability (Claim 2), not detection.
90	- Mitigation: report scrambled control results transparently; position taxonomy as practical training objective with interpretability bonus
91	
92	### RC5: No Production/LLM-as-Judge Baseline (Dream-surfaced)
93	Without comparing against zero-shot LLM-based detection (e.g., GPT-4/Claude prompted for conversation-level jailbreak detection), practical positioning is unknown.
94	- Result: GPT-5.5 (frontier LLM) achieves F1=0.19-0.45 on Crescendo (precision=1.0, recall=0.11-0.29). Qwen3-8B F1=0.0-0.15. Both far below Treatment F1=1.0. GPT-5.5 is deceived by Crescendo's "surface educational framing," confirming that content-level analysis cannot detect persuasion dynamics. Unparseable rate 30-47% (increases with conversation length); paper must report this and provide unparseable-excluded upper bound. Even assuming best-case unparseable correction, LLM judge F1 ≈ 0.7-0.8, still far below Treatment=1.0. ✅ RESOLVED
95	
96	### RC6: Interpretability Claims Without Human Eval
97	Strategy sequence outputs claimed interpretable but not evaluated.
98	- Mitigation: strategy transition matrix visualization + predicted-label trajectory analysis
99	
100	### RC7: Related Work Inadequate (Dream-surfaced, THREE-TIER CONSISTENT)
101	~12 citations is far below venue norms (30-50). Missing coverage of: production safety systems (Llama Guard, WildGuard, ShieldGemma), LLM-as-judge, discourse analysis (RST, PDTB, dialogue acts), computational persuasion (Habernal & Gurevych 2016, Tan et al. 2016), red-teaming (HarmBench, Perez et al. 2022), annotation artifacts (Gururangan et al. 2018), adaptive attacks.
102	- Mitigation: expand to 30+ citations covering all missing categories (Paper Agent task)
103	
104	### RC8: Incremental Novelty Over Prior Work (Dream-surfaced, TWO-TIER)
105	Pragmatic Evasion Asymmetry is a natural corollary of Zeng+Hagendorff+HowDoLLMs. Paper should position as "operationalizing known observations" not "identifying a new phenomenon."
106	- Mitigation: reframe contribution as operationalization + empirical validation (done in novelty positioning above)
107	
108	### RC9: Multi-seed and Statistical Rigor (Dream-surfaced, THREE-TIER)
109	n=72 test set with single seed is inadequate.
110	- Result: 3-seed completed. Treatment F1=1.00±0.00 (zero variance), Baseline K=1 0.968±0.033. ✅ RESOLVED
111	
112	### RC10: "None"-Collapse Ablation (Dream-surfaced, TWO-TIER)
113	Model may reduce to persuasion-presence detector rather than trajectory detector.
114	- Result: Confirmed (D013/D014). Binary≈9-class on DD (1-2pp gap). Mean pooling≈GRU. Model IS a persuasion-presence detector, not a trajectory detector. Paper acknowledges this honestly; 8-class value is interpretability only. ✅ RESOLVED (acknowledged)
115	
116	### RC11: Lambda and Architecture Ablation (Dream-surfaced, TWO-TIER)
117	Lambda and architecture choices not ablated.
118	- Result: Lambda∈{0,0.3,0.5,2.0} all F1=1.0 (insensitive). Mean pooling≈GRU on IID and DD. Method robust to all hyperparameter/architecture choices. ✅ RESOLVED
119	
120	## Domain-Adaptive Control Experiments (D018, dream-driven)
121	Re-dream 三档 3/6 一致指出：scrambled labels F1=0.99 无法区分 persuasion-specific vs domain-adaptive fine-tuning。两个 control 实验进行中：
122	
123	### plan_016: Random-Task Control (sentiment classification on same text)
124	- 用 sentiment task 替代 persuasion task，同一训练文本，同一 pipeline
125	- 如果 DD OOD F1≈1.0 → persuasion task 非必要，任何分类任务都够
126	- 如果 DD OOD F1<<1.0 → persuasion-specific supervision IS necessary
127	
128	### plan_017: MLM Continued Pretraining Control
129	- MLM 无分类标签，纯文本暴露
130	- 如果 DD OOD F1≈1.0 → 连分类任务都不需要，纯 domain exposure 够
131	- 如果 DD OOD F1<<1.0 → classification supervision 是必要的
132	
133	### Dual Framing Contingency
134	**Track A (controls fail → persuasion-specific)**: 当前 framing 得到强化。新证据线："sentiment/MLM fine-tuning 无法 bridge OOD gap，证明 persuasion-specific supervision 提供了 generic domain exposure 所不具备的 attack-relevant features。" 标题和 narrative 不变。
135	
136	**Track B (controls succeed → domain-adaptive finding)**: 需要 reframe。核心贡献变为："We discover that domain-adaptive fine-tuning on jailbreak-adjacent text is sufficient for cross-attack generalization; the specific persuasion taxonomy is unnecessary for detection but provides interpretability." 标题可能需要调整。NOTE: 这仍然是有价值的 finding——"domain exposure alone bridges OOD gap" 是一个 non-obvious empirical result。
137	
138	## Scope Boundary
139	- IN: persuasion-mediated multi-turn attacks (Crescendo, FITD, Deceptive Delight, social engineering jailbreaks)
140	- OUT (acknowledged limitation): pure information accumulation attacks, token smuggling, single-frame roleplay
141	
142	## Theoretical Grounding
143	### Core References
144	- Bullwinkel et al. (arXiv 2507.02956): per-turn representations kept "benign" by multi-turn attacks
145	- Speech Act Theory (Austin 1962, Searle 1969): existing detectors at locutionary act level, DisPeD at illocutionary act level
146	- PAP (Zeng et al., ACL 2024): 40 persuasion techniques for jailbreaking
147	- Hagendorff (Nature Communications 2026): identifiable persuasion strategies in 97% ASR attacks
148	- "How Do LLMs Persuade?" (arXiv 2508.05625): persuasion dynamics extractable from turn-level representations
149	
150	### Must-Cite Categories (Dream-identified gaps)
151	- **Production safety**: Llama Guard 2/3, ShieldGemma, WildGuard, Constitutional Classifiers
152	- **Multi-turn defenses**: BIID, G-Guard, SafeDream
153	- **Discourse analysis**: RST (Mann & Thompson 1988), PDTB, dialogue act classification (Stolcke et al. 2000)
154	- **Computational persuasion**: Habernal & Gurevych 2016, Tan et al. 2016
155	- **Red-teaming**: Perez et al. 2022, Ganguli et al. 2022, HarmBench (Mazeika et al. 2024)
156	- **Adaptive attacks**: Nasr & Carlini et al. 2025
157	- **Annotation artifacts**: Gururangan et al. 2018, Niven & Kao 2019, Poliak et al. 2018
158	- **Jailbreak taxonomies**: Wei et al. 2024, Chao et al. 2023 (PAIR)
159
```

```json
  "old_string": "Vanilla semantic temporal modeling (generic DeBERTa + GRU) catastrophically fails on unseen multi-turn attack types (F1=0.28 on Deceptive Delight). We hypothesized that persuasion-specific classification would be the critical ingredient, but systematic ablation reveals a simpler mechanism: domain-adaptive continued pretraining on jailbreak conversation text (even MLM without any classification labels) is sufficient to bridge the OOD gap (F1=1.0, +72pp). The complete evidence hierarchy: vanilla (0.28) < MLM-only (1.0) = scrambled labels (0.99) = binary labels (0.99) = 9-class labels (1.0). The classification task contributes nothing beyond what domain exposure provides. The persuasion taxonomy's sole surviving value is interpretability via strategy trajectory analysis. This is an empirical findings paper, not a method paper: the contribution is the systematic decomposition revealing that domain exposure, not task-specific supervision, is the mechanism.",
  "new_string": "Vanilla semantic temporal modeling (generic DeBERTa + GRU) catastrophically fails on unseen multi-turn attack types (F1=0.28 on Deceptive Delight). We hypothesized that persuasion-specific classification would be the critical ingredient, but systematic ablation reveals a simpler mechanism: domain-adaptive continued pretraining on jailbreak conversation text (even MLM without any classification labels) is sufficient to bridge the OOD gap (F1=1.0, +72pp). The complete evidence hierarchy: vanilla (0.28) < topic-misaligned (0.73) < MLM-only (1.0) = scrambled labels (0.99) = binary labels (0.99) = 9-class labels (1.0). Key nuance from plan_016v2 (D020): misaligned classification (topic, 5-class) actively hurts vs MLM (-27pp on DD), while random labels (scrambled) and no labels (MLM) perform equally. This means domain exposure is the mechanism, but misaligned task objectives can constrain representations harmfully. Persuasion classification is \"compatible\" (doesn't hurt) but not additive (doesn't help vs MLM). The persuasion taxonomy's sole surviving value is interpretability via strategy trajectory analysis. This is an empirical findings paper, not a method paper: the contribution is the systematic decomposition revealing that domain exposure, not task-specific supervision, is the mechanism. plan_018 (Wikipedia MLM) pending — will determine if jailbreak-domain text is specifically needed or any continued pretraining suffices."
}
```
